Build T:
```
def _build_conv_matrix(a):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; T=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
    idxs, poss = _get_conv_indices(n)
    for k in range(L):
       lo,hi=poss[k]
       if hi>lo:
          T[k, lo:hi] = a[idxs[k]]
    return T
```
This copies each row. Good.

Convolution functions:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p<<=1
    return p
def _fft_convolve2(x,y):
    nx=x.size; ny=y.size
    if nx==0 or ny==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    N=_next_pow2(nx+ny-1)
    fx=np.fft.rfft(x,N); fy=np.fft.rfft(y,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fx*fy,N)
    return c[:nx+ny-1]
def _fft_autoc(a): return _fft_convolve2(a,a)
```
For score: `c = _fft_autoc(a); c=np.where(c>0,c,0); m=np.max(c); s=np.sum(a); return inf if s<0.01 else 2*n*m/(s*s)`.
For normalized max1:
```
def _normalize_to_max1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: uniform
   c=_fft_autoc(a); c=np.maximum(c,0); m=c.max()
   if m>0: a=a/math.sqrt(m)
   return a
```

def _as_nonneg_array(seq):
    arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    if arr.size<2 or arr.sum()<1e-12:
       arr=np.ones(1024)/1024
    return arr
```

Line search function:

def _line_search_ratio(a, u, n_steps=20, t_max=1.0):
    S=a.sum(); M=1.0; best_ratio=S; best_a=a.copy()
    # include small grid, logarithmic? Use fractions: 1/n_steps ...1.
    for t in np.linspace(0.05, t_max, n_steps):
       at=(1-t)*a + t*u
       c=_fft_autoc(at); c=np.maximum(c,0); cmax=float(np.max(c))
       if cmax<=0: continue
       s=float(np.sum(at))
       ratio=s/math.sqrt(cmax)
       if ratio>best_ratio:
          best_ratio=ratio; best_a=at
    # also t=1.2? if best at t_max, try 1.2 maybe; but u may far. We'll include t up to1 only.
    return best_a, best_ratio
```

def _optimize_lp(a, deadline, verbose=True):
    a = _normalize_to_max1(a)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = _score(a)
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    stagnant=0
    base_sigma=0.1
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # maybe if long no improve, perturb best
        if time.monotonic()-last_improve > 30:
           # create perturbed variant
           sig=base_sigma*(0.5**stagnant?)
           ax = a * np.exp(clip(rng.normal(0,sig),-2,2)); ax=normalize
           # maybe run a few LP from ax
           ...
        u,raw,z = _lp_direction_ratio(a, time_limit=2.0)
        if u is not None:
            at, ratio = _line_search_ratio(a,u)
            if ratio > a.sum()*(1+1e-8):
                a = _normalize_to_max1(at)
                sc=_score(a)
                if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=now; stagnant=0
                else: # score may not improve due round? still a changed
                   pass
            else:
                stagnant +=1
        else:
            stagnant +=1
    return best_a,best_score
```

    rng...
    lr=0.25*a.size
    patience=100; it=0
    while time<deadline:
       c=_fft_autoc(a); M=c.max(); score=2*n*M (sum1)
       # weights: softmax over all c/T? Use top K for speed
       if c.size<=topK: idx=arange; else idx=argpartition(c,-topK)[topK:]
       vals=c[idx]; shift=vals-vals.max(); w=exp(T_soft*shift); w/=sum
       wfull=zeros; wfull[idx]=w
       # grad = 2*conv_first_n(wfull,a)
       grad=_conv_first_n(wfull,a)[:n]*2
       grad_proj=grad - np.mean(grad)
       update=-lr*grad_proj; update=clip(update,-5,5)
       a=a*np.exp(update); clip; normalize sum1
       if it%3: sc=_score; if sc<best: update best; lr*=1.01
       else patience...
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _make_sqrt_seq(n):
    a=np.empty(n)
    a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
       a[k]=a[k-1]*(k-0.5)/k
    return a
```

```
def _softmax_weights_normalized(c, T):
    c_min=float(np.min(c)); c_max=float(np.max(c)); rng=c_max-c_min
    if rng <1e-15: return np.ones(c.size)/c.size
    c_norm=(c-c_min)/rng
    c_shift=c_norm-1.0
    exp_c=np.exp(T*c_shift)
    s=exp_c.sum()
    if s<1e-300: ...
    return exp_c/s
```

def _optimize_soft(a, deadline, T_start=6.0, T_end=80.0, lr0=None):
    a = np.asarray(a,float).copy(); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: uniform; a=a/s
    n=a.size
    if lr0 is None: lr0=0.25*n
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score(a); best_score=np.min(best_score,evaluate? no just _score)
    it=0; last=0; patience=3000
    rng=np.random.RandomState(13)
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       frac=min(1.0,it/50000.0)
       T=T_start+(T_end-T_start)*frac
       lr=lr0/(1.0+0.005*it)
       c=_fft_autoc(a)
       w=_softmax_weights_normalized(c,T)
       grad=2.0*_convolve_first_n(w,a)
       grad=grad-np.mean(grad)
       update=-lr*grad; update=np.clip(update,-10,10)
       a=a*np.exp(update); a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
       s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: break
       a=a/s
       if it%5==0:
          sc=_score(a)
          if sc<best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); last=it; lr0=min(0.5*n,lr0*1.01)
          elif it-last>patience:
             a=best_a.copy(); noise=rng.standard_normal(n)*0.01; a=a+noise*a.mean(); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); a=a/s
             patience=max(1500,int(patience*1.3)); lr0=max(0.05*n,lr0*0.8)
    return best_a,best_score

Stagnation break: We can break after `stagnant > 25` to let soft. But if we break, `_optimize_lp` returns best. In propose after LP, run soft/hard. Good.

Now, one more analytic possibility: The LP with ratio may produce sequence with max C not at unique coefficient, but many; line search ratio uses exact max. Good.

Let's include a tiny final deterministic refinement: coordinate top-K local search? Maybe not.

Check `_convolve_first_n`: If x length L (2n-1), y length n, but for soft w length same as c from a (2n-1). Good.

In `_optimize_soft`, after update and normalization sum1, a values may become very small; exp overflow? update clipped ±10, a positive. Good.

In `_optimize_hard`, top-K weights: if K=16, c.size maybe small. Use `argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]`; if K>=c.size, all.

Potential issue: `_optimize_soft` uses `patience` and restarts with best; if best sum1. Good.

Let's write final code in one block. Ensure docstring top. No analysis outside.

We should not import evaluate_sequence. Use global in try.

Let's produce final.
</think>

```python
"""
LP-assisted local search for low-peak nonnegative step functions.

The main idea is to work with sequences normalized so that
max_j (a*a)_j = 1, and to maximize S = sum(a), since

    score = 2n * max_j (a*a)_j / S^2 = 2n / S^2.

At a current feasible point a, we solve a linear program that chooses a
candidate u maximizing a first-order linearization of S(a_t)/sqrt(max(a_t*a_t)),
where a_t = (1-t)a + t*u.  A line search then accepts genuinely improving
steps.  Fallback smooth mirror-descent and top-K active-set refinement are
used to polish and escape small plateaus.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

# Cache convolution-index structures for LP matrix construction.
_conv_index_cache = {}


def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _fft_convolve2(x, y):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0 or y.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    nout = x.size + y.size - 1
    N = _next_pow2(nout)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fx * fy, N)
    return c[:nout]


def _fft_autoc(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    return _fft_convolve2(a, a)


def _convolve_first_n(x, y):
    """Return first len(y) coefficients of convolution x*y."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0 or y.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out = _fft_convolve2(x, y)
    return out[:y.size]


def _score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_autoc(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _make_sqrt_sequence(n):
    """
    Classical polynomial square-root construction:
    coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        a[0] = 1.0
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (k - 0.5) / float(k)
    return a


def _sanitize_array(arr, fallback_n=1024):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        a = _make_sqrt_sequence(fallback_n)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = _make_sqrt_sequence(fallback_n)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        a = _make_sqrt_sequence(fallback_n)
        s = float(a.sum())
    return a


def _normalize_to_max1(a):
    """Scale a so that max(a*a) is exactly 1 up to floating error."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        a = _make_sqrt_sequence(1024)
        s = float(a.sum())
    c = _fft_autoc(a)
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        a = a / s
        return a
    a = a / math.sqrt(m)
    # Rescale c for convenience: returned a has max convolution 1.
    return a


def _get_conv_indices(n):
    if n in _conv_index_cache:
        return _conv_index_cache[n]

    L = 2 * n - 1
    idxs = []
    spans = []
    for k in range(L):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi >= lo:
            # For row k and columns lo..hi, coefficient is a[k-j].
            # When columns increase, k-j decreases.
            idx = np.arange(k - lo, k - hi - 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
            idxs.append(idx)
        else:
            idxs.append(np.array([], dtype=np.int64))
        spans.append((lo, hi + 1))

    _conv_index_cache[n] = (idxs, spans)
    return idxs, spans


def _build_lp_matrix(a, a2):
    """
    Build A for constraints 2*(a*u)_k - z <= C_k.
    Columns 0..n-1 are for u, last column is for z.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:, -1] = -1.0

    idxs, spans = _get_conv_indices(n)
    two_a2 = 2.0 * a2
    for k in range(L):
        lo, hi = spans[k]
        if hi > lo:
            A[k, lo:hi] = two_a2[idxs[k]]
    return A


def _lp_direction(a, gamma, time_limit=1.2, ub=None, ub_z=4.0):
    """
    Solve linear program:
        minimize -1^T u + gamma*z
        subject to 2*(a*u)_k - z <= C_k, u >= 0, z in [0, ub_z].

    Returns (u, raw_value, z) or None.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None

    # Ensure max convolution is exactly 1, so baseline is clean.
    c = _fft_autoc(a)
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return None
    a = a / math.sqrt(m)
    c = c / m
    S = float(a.sum())
    gamma = float(abs(gamma))

    if ub is None:
        maxa = float(np.max(a)) if n else 0.0
        ub = max(2.0, 2.0 * maxa + 1.0)
    ub = min(float(ub), 20.0)

    A = _build_lp_matrix(a, c)
    b = c + 1e-7

    cobj = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cobj[:n] = -1.0
    cobj[n] = gamma

    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, ub))
    bounds.append((0.0, ub_z))

    try:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": float(time_limit)},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not bool(getattr(res, "success", False)):
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n + 1:
        return None

    u = np.clip(x[:n], 0.0, ub)
    z = float(np.clip(x[n], 0.0, ub_z))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return None

    raw = float(np.sum(u) - gamma * z)
    if not math.isfinite(raw):
        return None

    return u, raw, z


def _line_search(a, u, n_ts=14, t_max=1.0):
    """
    Line search along a_t = (1-t)a + t u.
    Returns best a_t and best normalized ratio S / sqrt(maxconv).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = a.copy()

    base_c = _fft_autoc(a)
    base_c = np.where(base_c > 0.0, base_c, 0.0)
    base_m = float(base_c.max())
    if base_m <= 0.0:
        base_m = 1.0
    best_ratio = float(a.sum()) / math.sqrt(base_m)

    if n_ts < 1:
        n_ts = 1
    if n_ts == 1:
        ts = np.array([t_max], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        ts = np.linspace(0.005, float(t_max), max(2, int(n_ts)))

    for t in ts:
        t = float(np.clip(t, 0.0, 2.0))
        if t == 0.0:
            continue
        at = (1.0 - t) * a + t * u
        at = np.where(np.isfinite(at), at, 0.0)
        at = np.clip(at, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(at.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            continue
        c = _fft_autoc(at)
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        cm = float(c.max())
        if cm <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(cm):
            continue
        ratio = s / math.sqrt(cm)
        if ratio > best_ratio:
            best_ratio = ratio
            best_a = at.copy()

    return best_a, best_ratio


def _optimize_lp(a, deadline):
    """
    Repeated LP linearization for cycles.
    """
    a = _normalize_to_max1(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(a)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(20240512)
    gamma_factors = [0.50, 0.45, 0.55, 0.40, 0.60, 0.35, 0.425]
    fi = 0
    stagnant = 0
    last_improve = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        improved = False
        S = float(a.sum())

        for _g in range(min(3, len(gamma_factors))):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            gf = gamma_factors[fi % len(gamma_factors)]
            fi += 1
            gamma = 0.5 * gf * S

            sol = _lp_direction(a, gamma, time_limit=1.0)
            if sol is None:
                continue

            u, raw, z = sol
            # current raw baseline is S - gamma
            if raw <= S - gamma - 1e-9:
                continue

            at, ratio = _line_search(a, u, n_ts=14, t_max=1.0)
            current_ratio = S
            if ratio > current_ratio * (1.0 + 1e-9):
                an = _normalize_to_max1(at)
                sc = _score_fast(an)
                if math.isfinite(sc):
                    a = an
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                        last_improve = time.monotonic()
                        improved = True
                        stagnant = 0
                        break

        if improved:
            continue

        stagnant += 1

        if stagnant >= 4 and stagnant % 4 == 0:
            # Perturb from best, not from possibly noisy current point.
            sig = 0.035 + 0.02 / float(stagnant + 1)
            ax = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n))
            ax = np.clip(ax, 0.0, 1000.0)
            ax = _normalize_to_max1(ax)
            scx = _score_fast(ax)
            # Allow mildly worse exploration, but not absurd.
            if math.isfinite(scx) and scx <= best_score + 0.08:
                a = ax
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
            stagnant = 0

        if stagnant > 45:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def _softmax_weights_normalized(c, T):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c_min = float(np.min(c))
    c_max = float(np.max(c))
    rng = c_max - c_min
    if rng < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(c.size)
    c_norm = (c - c_min) / rng
    c_shift = c_norm - 1.0
    exp_c = np.exp(T * c_shift)
    s = float(exp_c.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = c_norm >= 1.0 - 1e-10
        nmax = int(mask.sum())
        if nmax == 0:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w[mask] = 1.0 / float(nmax)
        return w
    return exp_c / s


def _optimize_soft(a, deadline, T_start=6.0, T_end=120.0, lr0=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s < 1e-12:
        a = _make_sqrt_sequence(max(n, 1024))
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s

    if lr0 is None:
        lr0 = 0.25 * n

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(a)
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    patience = 4000
    rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        frac = min(1.0, it / 60000.0)
        T = T_start + (T_end - T_start) * frac
        lr = lr0 / (1.0 + 0.004 * it)

        c = _fft_autoc(a)
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        w = _softmax_weights_normalized(c, T)
        grad = 2.0 * _convolve_first_n(w, a)
        grad = grad - np.mean(grad)

        update = -lr * grad
        update = np.clip(update, -10.0, 10.0)
        a = a * np.exp(update)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-12:
            a = best_a.copy()
            break
        a = a / s

        if it % 4 == 0:
            sc = _score_fast(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_improve = it
                lr0 = min(0.5 * n, lr0 * 1.005)
            elif it - last_improve > patience:
                a = best_a.copy()
                noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.008
                a = a + noise * a.mean()
                a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-12:
                    a = a / s
                patience = max(2000, int(patience * 1.35))
                lr0 = max(0.05 * n, lr0 * 0.8)

    return best_a, best_score


def _optimize_hard(a, deadline, K=18, T_local=4.0, lr0=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s < 1e-12:
        a = _make_sqrt_sequence(max(n, 1024))
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s

    if lr0 is None:
        lr0 = 0.4 * n

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(a)
    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    patience = 3000
    rng = np.random.RandomState(99991)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        lr = lr0 / (1.0 + 0.003 * it)

        c = _fft_autoc(a)
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)

        if c.size <= K:
            top_idx = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

        vals = c[top_idx]
        shift = vals - float(vals.max())
        wtop = np.exp(T_local * shift)
        ws = float(wtop.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0:
            wtop = np.ones_like(wtop) / float(wtop.size)
        else:
            wtop = wtop / ws

        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        for i, k in enumerate(top_idx):
            w[int(k)] = wtop[i]

        grad = 2.0 * _convolve_first_n(w, a)
        grad = grad - np.mean(grad)

        update = -lr * grad
        update = np.clip(update, -10.0, 10.0)
        a = a * np.exp(update)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-12:
            a = best_a.copy()
            break
        a = a / s

        if it % 3 == 0:
            sc = _score_fast(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_improve = it
                lr0 = min(0.8 * n, lr0 * 1.004)
            elif it - last_improve > patience:
                a = best_a.copy()
                noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * 0.004
                a = a + noise * a.mean()
                a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-12:
                    a = a / s
                patience = max(1500, int(patience * 1.4))
                lr0 = max(0.1 * n, lr0 * 0.8)

    return best_a, best_score


def _to_output_list(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    out = []
    for x in a:
        try:
            xf = float(x)
        except Exception:
            xf = 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(xf):
            xf = 0.0
        if xf < 0.0:
            xf = 0.0
        if xf > 1000.0:
            xf = 1000.0
        out.append(xf)
    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        hard_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 25.0
    except Exception:
        hard_deadline = t0 + 2375.0

    np.random.seed(int(seed))

    candidates = []

    # Prefer the already discovered global height_sequence_1 if present.
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        if hs is not None:
            arr = _sanitize_array(hs, fallback_n=1024)
            if arr.size > 0:
                candidates.append(("global_hs1", arr))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Strong analytic fallback / diversity: sqrt(1/(1-z)) truncated.
    for ninit in [1024]:
        arr = _make_sqrt_sequence(ninit)
        candidates.append((f"sqrt_{ninit}", arr))

    # If time allows, also try a larger sqrt init, but do not optimize it
    # heavily unless needed.
    if hard_deadline - t0 > 1500:
        arr = _make_sqrt_sequence(2048)
        candidates.append(("sqrt_2048", arr))

    # Score all initial candidates.
    for name, arr in candidates:
        sc = _score_fast(arr)
        print(f"[init] {name}: n={arr.size}, score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            candidates[-1] = (name, arr, sc)

    # Keep list of (name, arr, score)
    scored = []
    for item in candidates:
        if len(item) == 3:
            scored.append(item)
    scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[2])

    if not scored:
        arr = _make_sqrt_sequence(1024)
        arr_list = _to_output_list(arr)
        try:
            real = float(evaluate_sequence(arr_list))
        except Exception:
            real = _score_fast(arr)
        return arr_list

    best_name, best_arr, best_score = scored[0]
    print(f"[best init] {best_name}: {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Main LP-driven optimization for the best few candidates.
    used_names = set()
    lp_tries = 0
    max_lp_tries = 3 if best_score < 1.51 else 2

    for name, arr, sc in scored:
        if lp_tries >= max_lp_tries:
            break
        if name in used_names:
            continue

        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 250:
            break

        n = arr.size
        if n > 2500:
            continue

        # Leave enough time for polishing.
        lp_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 180.0, time.monotonic() + 1200.0)
        if lp_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            break

        print(f"[LP] trying {name}, n={n}, current={sc:.8f}, budget until {lp_deadline - time.monotonic():.0f}s", flush=True)
        try:
            new_arr, new_score = _optimize_lp(arr, lp_deadline)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[LP] exception: {e}", flush=True)
            new_arr, new_score = arr, sc

        if math.isfinite(new_score):
            used_names.add(name)
            lp_tries += 1
            if new_score < best_score:
                best_name, best_arr, best_score = name, new_arr, new_score
                print(f"[LP improved] {name}: {new_score:.8f}", flush=True)
            else:
                print(f"[LP no improvement] {name}: {new_score:.8f}", flush=True)

            # Keep several polished candidates for final validation.
            scored.append((name + "_lp", new_arr, new_score))

    # Smooth refinement phase.
    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 300.0:
        polish_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 100.0, time.monotonic() + 300.0)
        if polish_deadline > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            print(f"[Soft] polishing {best_name}", flush=True)
            try:
                soft_arr, soft_score = _optimize_soft(best_arr, polish_deadline)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Soft] exception: {e}", flush=True)
                soft_arr, soft_score = best_arr, best_score

            if math.isfinite(soft_score):
                if soft_score < best_score:
                    best_name, best_arr, best_score = best_name + "_soft", soft_arr, soft_score
                    print(f"[Soft improved] {best_name}: {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)
                else:
                    print(f"[Soft no improvement] {best_score:.8f} vs {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)
                scored.append((best_name + "_soft", soft_arr, soft_score))

    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 180.0:
        hard_deadline2 = min(hard_deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 220.0)
        if hard_deadline2 > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            print(f"[Hard] polishing {best_name}", flush=True)
            try:
                hard_arr, hard_score = _optimize_hard(best_arr, hard_deadline2, K=20)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Hard] exception: {e}", flush=True)
                hard_arr, hard_score = best_arr, best_score

            if math.isfinite(hard_score):
                if hard_score < best_score:
                    best_name, best_arr, best_score = best_name + "_hard", hard_arr, hard_score
                    print(f"[Hard improved] {best_name}: {best_score:.8f}", flush=True)
                else:
                    print(f"[Hard no improvement] {best_score:.8f} vs {hard_score:.8f}", flush=True)
                scored.append((best_name + "_hard", hard_arr, hard_score))

    # Sort final candidate pool.
    scored = list(set(scored))
    scored_sorted = []
    for name, arr, sc in scored:
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            scored_sorted.append((name, arr, sc))
    scored_sorted.sort(key=lambda x: x[2])

    # Validate top candidates with the actual evaluator.
    final_pool = scored_sorted[:8]
    if not any(nm == best_name for nm, _, _ in final_pool):
        final_pool.append((best_name, best_arr, best_score))

    true_best_list = None
    true_best_score = np.inf

    for name, arr, est in final_pool:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 5:
            break
        arr_list = _to_output_list(arr)
        try:
            real = float(evaluate_sequence(arr_list))
        except Exception:
            real = est
        if math.isfinite(real):
            print(f"[validate] {name}: estimated={est:.8f}, actual={real:.8f}, n={arr.size}", flush=True)
            if real < true_best_score:
                true_best_score = real
                true_best_list = arr_list
        else:
            print(f"[validate] {name}: invalid", flush=True)

    if true_best_list is None:
        true_best_list = _to_output_list(best_arr)

    print(f"[done] best actual score: {true_best_score:.10f}", flush=True)
    return true_best_list
```
